Memory is left\n//   untouched so that the pages can be first-touched (and hence NUMA-placed)\n//   by the thread that will later work on them.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct AlignedDeleter {\n    void operator()(T* p) const { std::free(p); }\n};\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nusing AlignedArray = std::unique_ptr<T[], AlignedDeleter<T>>;\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nAlignedArray<T> allocArray(const size_t n) {\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(T) + TOUCH_GRANULARITY - 1) / TOUCH_GRANULARITY) * TOUCH_GRANULARITY;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(TOUCH_GRANULARITY, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Failed to allocate %zu bytes\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n#ifdef MADV_HUGEPAGE\n    madvise(p, bytes, MADV_HUGEPAGE);\n#endif\n    return AlignedArray<T>(static_cast<T*>(p));\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: bindThreads\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Pins the OpenMP threads to distinct CPUs, spread over all CPUs the process\n//   is allowed to run on. Stable placement is what makes the NUMA-aware first\n//   touch below pay off. Skipped if the user expressed a placement policy or if\n//   the run is oversubscribed.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid bindThreads(const int numThreads) {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr) {\n        return;\n    }\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(c, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(c);\n        }\n    }\n    if (static_cast<int>(cpus.size()) < numThreads) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const int cpu = cpus[(tid * cpus.size()) / static_cast<size_t>(numThreads)];\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(mask), &mask);\n    }\n#else\n    (void)numThreads;\n#endif\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: touchBlocked / touchInterleaved\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   First-touch helpers. touchBlocked hands each thread the contiguous block of\n//   elements it will later read (matching the non-zero balanced partitioning),\n//   while touchInterleaved distributes pages round-robin over the threads, which\n//   approximates a NUMA interleaved placement for the randomly accessed vector.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid touchBlocked(T* data, const size_t n, const int numThreads) {\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(numThreads)\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t begin = (n * tid) / static_cast<size_t>(numThreads);\n        const size_t end = (n * (tid + 1)) / static_cast<size_t>(numThreads);\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            data[i] = T{};\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid touchInterleaved(T* data, const size_t n, const int numThreads) {\n    const size_t pageElems = TOUCH_GRANULARITY / sizeof(T);\n    const size_t numPages = (n + pageElems - 1) / pageElems;\n#pragma omp parallel for num_threads(numThreads) schedule(static, 1)\n    for (size_t p = 0; p < numPages; ++p) {\n        const size_t begin = p * pageElems;\n        const size_t end = std::min(n, begin + pageElems);\n        for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n            data[i] = T{};\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: partitionRows\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Splits the rows into one contiguous range per thread such that every thread\n//   receives (close to) the same number of non-zero elements. This keeps the\n//   memory bound kernel balanced even when the row lengths vary.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid partitionRows(const index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t dim, const index_t nnz, const int numThreads) {\n    g_rowStart.assign(static_cast<size_t>(numThreads) + 1, dim);\n    g_rowStart[0] = 0;\n    for (int t = 1; t < numThreads; ++t) {\n        const index_t target =\n            static_cast<index_t>((static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) * static_cast<uint64_t>(t)) / static_cast<uint64_t>(numThreads));\n        const index_t* pos = std::lower_bound(rowDelimiters, rowDelimiters + dim + 1, target);\n        g_rowStart[t] = static_cast<index_t>(pos - rowDelimiters);\n    }\n    g_rowStart[numThreads] = dim;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel for a single thread's row range. The accumulation order within a row is\n// kept identical to the serial version, so results are bit-for-bit reproducible.\nstatic inline void spmvRows(const double* __restrict val, const index_t* __restrict cols,\n                            const index_t* __restrict rowDelimiters, const double* __restrict vec,\n                            const index_t rowBegin, const index_t rowEnd, double* __restrict out) {\n    for (index_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    (void)dim;\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(g_numThreads)\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        spmvRows(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, g_rowStart[tid], g_rowStart[tid + 1], out);\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n    printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n    printf(\"Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\\n\", \n           nItems, 100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nItems) / (numRows * numRows)));\n    printf(\"Iterations: %u\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Max value: %.2f\\n\", maxVal);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    g_numThreads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (g_numThreads > static_cast<int>(numRows)) {\n        g_numThreads = static_cast<int>(numRows);\n    }\n    if (g_numThreads < 1) {\n        g_numThreads = 1;\n    }\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", g_numThreads);\n    bindThreads(g_numThreads);\n\n    // Allocate and initialize data structures\n    auto h_val = allocArray<double>(nItems);                   // Non-zero values\n    auto h_cols = allocArray<index_t>(nItems);                 // Column indices\n    auto h_rowDelimiters = allocArray<index_t>(numRows + 1u);  // Row delimiters\n    auto h_vec = allocArray<double>(numRows);                  // Dense vector\n    auto h_out = allocArray<double>(numRows);                  // Output vector\n\n    // NUMA-aware first touch: the streamed matrix arrays are distributed in the\n    // same blocks the threads will process, while the randomly indexed dense\n    // vector is static_cast<size_t>(numRows) + 1, g_numThreads);\n    touchInterleaved(h_vec.get(), numRows, g_numThreads);\n\n    printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n    fill(h_vec.get(), numRows, maxVal);\n    fill(h_val.get(), nItems, maxVal);\n    initRandomMatrix(h_cols.get(), h_rowDelimiters.get(), nItems, numRows);\n\n    // Balance the rows over the threads by number of non-zeroes\n    partitionRows(h_rowDelimiters.get(), numRows, nItems, g_numThreads);\n\n    // First touch the output vector according to the final row partitioning\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(g_numThreads)\n spans all iterations so that fork/join overhead is\n    // paid once instead of per iteration.\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(g_numThreads)\n    {\n        const size_t tid = duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    const double gflops = (2.0 * nItems * iterations) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    const double avgTime = duration.count() / static_cast<double>(iterations);\n    \n    printf(\"Average time per The benchmark is parallelized with OpenMP and produces bit-identical results to the original serial code (verified by comparing `-r` output hashes against a reconstructed serial reference for several sizes: n=2, 1023×s7, 4096, 8192×s13 — all hashes match exactly).\n\n## What changed in `spmv/spmv.cpp`\n\n**Kernel** — `spmvRows()` holds the per-thread row range loop; the in-row accumulation order is untouched, so every output element is computed exactly as before (no `simd reduction`, which would have reordered the FP adds and broken the bit-exact FNV hash used by `print_results`).\n\n**Load balancing** — `partitionRows()` gives each thread a contiguous row range holding an equal share of the *non-zeroes* (binary search over `rowDelimiters`), rather than an equal number of rows. Rows are independent, so there is no synchronization in the kernel at all.\n\n**Timed loop** — one `omp parallel` region spans all iterations, so fork/join is paid once. An `asm volatile(\"\" ::: \"memory\")` per iteration keeps the loop-invariant computation from being hoisted.\n\n**NUMA (this is the bulk of the win on a 2-socket box)** — arrays moved from `std::vector` (which zero-fills serially, landing every page on one node) to uninitialized 2 MiB-aligned allocations that are first-touched by the thread that will later use them: `val`/`cols`/`rowDelimiters`/`out` in the same blocks the kernel processes, and the randomly gathered dense vector `vec` round-robin per page to approximate interleaving. `MADV_HUGEPAGE` cuts TLB pressure on the streamed arrays.\n\n**Affinity** — `bindThreads()` pins each thread to a distinct CPU spread across the process's allowed set (respects `taskset`/cgroup masks, skipped when `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES` is set or when oversubscribed), so the first-touch placement actually holds.\n\nInitialization (`fill`, `initRandomMatrix`) stays serial — it is driven by `rand()`, and parallelizing it would change the matrix. It is not part of the timed region.\n\n`CMakeLists.txt` links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX` and `Threads::Threads`; executable name unchanged.\n\n## Scaling (AMD EPYC 7763 ×2, 128 cores / 256 threads, `-n 16384 -i 200`)\n\n| threads | 1 | 8 | 32 | 64 | 128 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| GFLOPS | 1.28 | 5.61 | 21.6 | 45.5 | 87.3 | ~145 |\n\n≈113× over the single-thread baseline; at that point the kernel is DRAM-bandwidth bound.\n\nOne pre-existing wart I left alone: timing uses millisecond resolution, so small runs (e.g. the default `-n 1024`) now finish in 0 ms and print `inf GFLOPS`. Fixing it would change the reported output format, so I didn't — say the word if you'd like it switched to a higher-resolution measurement."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":192,"cache_read_input_tokens":47399,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":192},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"82029c8c-1725-4297-99cf-9d5bba1aae0e","uuid":"c7465637-ce1d-4544-bbce-6f0a27039bd4","timestamp":"2026-09-03T15:57:43.158Z","request_id":"req_011CegnEx552Mqh1HQJBpHQc"}
